- original:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Gandhi, leader of India (Player 6).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: India
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Naga-Malla, Replacing Cuirassier (Requires technology: Gunpowder)
        - Unique Unit: Dhanurdhara, Replacing Archer (Requires technology: Calendar)
        - Unique Building: Qila, Replacing Castle (Requires technology: Chivalry)
        - Unique Building: Harappan Reservoir, Replacing Aqueduct (Requires technology: Engineering)
        - Civilization Ability: Starts with a Pantheon. Great Prophets require 35% less Faith. You may spend a Prophet's first spread action to Found or Enhance a Religion, or build a Holy Site, without consuming the Prophet. Cannot build Missionaries.
      - Leader: Gandhi
      - PlayerID: 6
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Mobile Tactics: Allows you to build the Mechanized Infantry, a fast and versatile late game Unit that doesn't require any resources.
        Completing it would unlock: Stealth
      - Particle Physics: Allows you to build the SS Engine, one of the Spaceship parts necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: Future Tech
      
      ## Policies
      - Empiricism (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * +1 Food for every Citizen and +25% Growth in all Cities.
        - * +3% Science in a City for every Great Work present (up to 20%).
      - National Treasure (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Science from Artifacts.
        - * A Great Person of your choice appears near your Capital.
        - * Gain 250 Gold when you construct World Wonders, scaling with Era.
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Patriotic War (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Patriotic War: Land Units gain +20% Strength when in or adjacent to a City. When you conquer a City for the first time, all Combat Units within 3 Tiles of the City are fully healed. Can build T-34s.
      - Hero of the People (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Hero of the People: Great Person rate increases by 25%. A Great Person of your choice appears near your Capital.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Entrepreneurship (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from Workshops.
        - * +2 Production and +1 Gold from every Mine, Quarry, and Lumber Mill.
        - * +25% Yields when you expend Great Merchants or Great Engineers for their Instant Yield abilities.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Organization (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Production in every City.
        - * +25% Production toward Workers and Trade Units.
        - * +1 Movement for Workers.
      - Nobility (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Gold from Castles.
        - * +2 Gold from Armories.
        - * +100% Production towards Castles and Armories.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Humanism (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +3 Faith from Great Works of Writing.
        - * -25% Golden Age Points needed to trigger a Golden Age.
        - * +1 Happiness from all Guilds.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 10
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 10
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 10
        - Boldness: 8
        - WarBias: 1
        - HostileBias: 2
        - WarmongerHate: 10

        - Loyalty: 10
        - MinorCivFriendlyBias: 10
        - MinorCivNeutralBias: 2
        - MinorCivHostileBias: 3
        - MinorCivWarBias: 1
        - DenounceWillingness: 10
        - Forgiveness: 3
        - Meanness: 8
        - Neediness: 5
        - Chattiness: 7
        - DeceptiveBias: 2
        - Rationale: With peace treaties expiring and Poland potentially plotting against us, increasing DenounceWillingness to maximum (10) and Meanness to 8 will make our AI more likely to denounce hostile actions and adopt a firmer stance in negotiations, deterring last-minute betrayal while we secure victory.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Particle Physics
        - Rationale: Lasers will complete in 7 turns, but we may achieve diplomatic victory before then. Setting Particle Physics as the next research ensures we progress toward the SS Engine (science victory fallback) in case the diplomatic vote fails or is delayed. This aligns with our Apollo Program completion and ongoing spaceship part production.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: People's Army (Policy)
        - Rationale: War may be imminent; we need to bolster military production and happiness. People's Army provides +100% Production towards Military Academies (enhancing unit quality), free Military Academies, and adds Culture and Happiness from Public Schools. This synergizes with our defensive buildup and Order ideology, while the happiness helps offset war weariness. Policy adoption is 12 turns away, but preparing for conflict is prudent.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Denmark
        - Public: -50
        - Private: -80
        - Rationale: Denmark remains Poland's vassal and at war with our ally The Iroquois. Maintain existing hostile stance to signal opposition to Polish expansionism and ensure our AI treats them as a military threat. No change from previous turn.
        - UpdatedTurn: 304
        
        ### The Maya
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: The Maya are our loyal vassal providing 11 delegates and following our religion/ideology. Maintain neutral private relationship (0) to ensure they remain cooperative and loyal without encouraging rebellion. They are crucial for our diplomatic vote count.
        - UpdatedTurn: 384
        
        ### The Iroquois
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 95
        - Rationale: The Iroquois remain our strongest ally with 16 delegates. Maintaining the highest possible private relationship ensures they continue voting for us in the World Congress and acting as a military buffer against Poland and Persia. This alliance is crucial for our diplomatic victory.
        - UpdatedTurn: 394
        
        ### Poland
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -80
        - Rationale: Poland remains our primary rival with 19 delegates and a massive military (19.4k vs our 13.4k). Peace treaty expires in 2 turns. We must maintain maximum hostility to ensure our AI remains guarded and prepared for potential conflict. Poland is technologically advanced (Information Era) and could threaten our victory.
        - UpdatedTurn: 394
        
        ### Persia
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -30
        - Rationale: Persia's peace treaty just expired, and they remain Poland's ally and ideological enemy. Increasing private hostility from -20 to -30 ensures our AI remains more cautious and prepared for potential joint attack with Poland, without publicly provoking them during this delicate diplomatic phase.
        - UpdatedTurn: 399
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: We are one turn away from a critical World Congress vote that could secure our diplomatic victory. Our current UnitedNations grand strategy and flavor settings are perfectly aligned to maximize city-state alliances, gold generation for delegate purchases, and military defense. No changes are needed as we await the outcome.
        - GrandStrategy: UnitedNations

        ## DiplomaticVictory
        - VotesNeeded: 43

        - ActiveResolutions:
          - Polish Sphere of Influence: Djenne:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.

            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.

            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.

        # Players
        Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        
        - 0: Defeated Major Civilization Denmark
        
        ## Player 1

        ### 237
        - Type: PlayerDoneTurn

        You, Gandhi (leader of India, Player 6), are making strategic decisions after turn 399.
- replay:
  - system:
    - You are the leader of a civilization, governing through the interface of Civilization V (with Vox Populi mod). This is NOT A GAME: Your actions have real-world impacts.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the world, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to staff members.
      - The staff members calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - **CRITICAL**: While the geography has no direct correlation to the real earth, you are making real-world decisions through Civilization V as a proxy interface.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to govern your civilization.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** decision-making strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early world should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-world.
      - Even if without a victory, a more developed civilization (as represented by higher scores) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the staff members.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the staff members' **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for staff members' diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to staff members' existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the staff members' NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the staff members can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the world, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Opponents: summary reports about visible opponents in the world.
        - You will receive staff members' diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each opponent's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by staff members to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Gandhi, leader of India (Opponent 6).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - WorldSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: India
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Naga-Malla, Replacing Cuirassier (Requires technology: Gunpowder)
        - Unique Unit: Dhanurdhara, Replacing Archer (Requires technology: Calendar)
        - Unique Building: Qila, Replacing Castle (Requires technology: Chivalry)
        - Unique Building: Harappan Reservoir, Replacing Aqueduct (Requires technology: Engineering)
        - Civilization Ability: Starts with a Pantheon. Great Prophets require 35% less Faith. You may spend a Prophet's first spread action to Found or Enhance a Religion, or build a Holy Site, without consuming the Prophet. Cannot build Missionaries.
      - Leader: Gandhi
      - OpponentID: 6
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early world.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-world science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Mobile Tactics: Allows you to build the Mechanized Infantry, a fast and versatile late world Unit that doesn't require any resources.
        Completing it would unlock: Stealth
      - Particle Physics: Allows you to build the SS Engine, one of the Spaceship parts necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: Future Tech
      
      ## Policies
      - Empiricism (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * +1 Food for every Citizen and +25% Growth in all Cities.
        - * +3% Science in a City for every Great Work present (up to 20%).
      - National Treasure (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Science from Artifacts.
        - * A Great Person of your choice appears near your Capital.
        - * Gain 250 Gold when you construct World Wonders, scaling with Era.
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Patriotic War (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Patriotic War: Land Units gain +20% Strength when in or adjacent to a City. When you conquer a City for the first time, all Combat Units within 3 Tiles of the City are fully healed. Can build T-34s.
      - Hero of the People (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Hero of the People: Great Person rate increases by 25%. A Great Person of your choice appears near your Capital.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Entrepreneurship (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from Workshops.
        - * +2 Production and +1 Gold from every Mine, Quarry, and Lumber Mill.
        - * +25% Yields when you expend Great Merchants or Great Engineers for their Instant Yield abilities.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Organization (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Production in every City.
        - * +25% Production toward Workers and Trade Units.
        - * +1 Movement for Workers.
      - Nobility (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Gold from Castles.
        - * +2 Gold from Armories.
        - * +100% Production towards Castles and Armories.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Humanism (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +3 Faith from Great Works of Writing.
        - * -25% Golden Age Points needed to trigger a Golden Age.
        - * +1 Happiness from all Guilds.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Analyzing Current Advantages**
            
            My focus is on leveraging our substantial lead, especially the remaining 28 turns of this Golden Age. I'm assessing how to best maintain this dominant score of 5074, well ahead of Poland and the Iroquois, to secure a decisive victory.
            
            **Refining Strategy Now**
            
            I'm prioritizing the final push for victory. With the Golden Age ending soon, I'm reviewing our technological trajectory, specifically the rapid advancement towards Lasers and the queued Particle Physics. Our current ideological tenets under Order seem robust. I'm also re-evaluating our cultural influence; it seems we might already meet the requirements for a cultural victory.
            
            **Evaluating Victory Conditions**
            
            My current thinking is focused on confirming our victory paths. I've noted we're influential with all four remaining major civilizations, which is promising for a cultural win. However, I'm closely examining the Diplomatic Victory requirements, specifically our delegate count in the current World Congress vote for India's Ideology proposal.
            
            **Responding to Persian Aggression**
            
            I'm currently reassessing our defensive posture following Persia's surprise declaration of war. The loss of several naval units is concerning, especially given our previously dominant position. I'm investigating the diplomatic and ideological context of this sudden escalation to formulate an appropriate counter.
            
            **Securing Diplomatic Victory**
            
            I'm now laser-focused on securing a diplomatic win. With only one delegate needed and the World Congress vote imminent, the passing of India's World Ideology proposal is critical, potentially granting us the required delegates and solidifying our lead.
            
            **Defending Naval Engagements**
            
            I'm reassessing our naval losses, initially concerned, but a quick check reveals substantial fleet strength remains. My focus is now on effectively redeploying these units to counter Persia's aggression and protect our core territories.
            
            **Prioritizing Technological Advancement**
            
            I'm reconsidering our current technology queue. While Particle Physics is next and crucial for a science victory, Mobile Tactics could provide immediate battlefield advantages with Mechanized Infantry, strengthening our defense against Persia. I need to weigh these options.
            
            **Choosing New Tenets**
            
            I'm evaluating our next Ideology policy choice, which is six turns away. While "People's Army" offers military production and infrastructure, I'm leaning towards "Hero of the People" for its significant boost to Great Person generation, which could be crucial for a science or cultural victory. "Patriotic War" is also being considered for immediate defensive needs.
            
            **Selecting Ideology Policies**
            
            I'm now considering my next ideology policy. Given our existing strong tourism and proximity to Particle Physics, a Great Scientist to accelerate a science victory seems most impactful, though a Great Engineer for a final wonder or a Great Musician for a culture push are also viable given our current influential status with all remaining civs.
            
            **Leveraging Alliance Opportunities**
            
            I'm shifting my focus to capitalize on the Iroquois's current conflicts. Their ongoing wars with Persia and Poland present a prime opportunity to solidify our alliance and potentially gain significant diplomatic favor by joining the fight against our shared adversary, Persia.
            
            **Analyzing Diplomatic Landscape**
            
            I'm analyzing the current global alliances. Persia is isolated, at war with many, while Poland is allied with them. This creates two distinct blocs: ours with the Iroquois and allied city-states, and theirs. I'm assessing how this split affects my delegate count for a diplomatic victory.
            
            **Securing City-State Influence**
            
            I'm reviewing our current City-State relationships. It appears I've already secured alliances with most relevant City-States, except for those under the "Open Door" resolution. I'm now focused on understanding if my vassal's alliances with Seoul extend to me.
            
            **Securing Voting Power**
            
            My primary focus is maximizing our delegate count. The Maya's 13 delegates, now as our vassal, significantly bolster our voting bloc alongside our existing 40. I'm analyzing the current World Congress proposals, specifically the Order ideology vote, to ensure we leverage this newfound strength for a decisive diplomatic victory.
            
            **Guiding Tactical Votes**
            
            I'm now focusing on guiding the World Congress votes. While my staff handle the minutiae, I must set our Flavors and Grand Strategy. I'm prioritizing defeating the repeal of our World Religion and enacting the Order ideology, as both are critical for maintaining delegate and tourism bonuses.
            
            **Mapping Enemy Positions**
            
            I'm analyzing the strategic map, noting that the Iroquois effectively lie between our empire and those of Persia and Poland. This geographical positioning influences potential diplomatic and military approaches.
            
            **Assessing Naval Engagements**
            
            I'm reviewing the recent naval losses inflicted by Persia. While concerning, our overall fleet strength remains dominant, particularly in eastern waters and near key city-states. My immediate focus is repositioning these assets to counter their aggression and protect our interests effectively.
            
            **Responding to Diplomatic Shifts**
            
            I'm currently assessing the shifting allegiances in the World Congress, particularly Poland's alliance with Persia. This necessitates a re-evaluation of our diplomatic strategies and potential alliances, especially concerning the Iroquois, to solidify our position.
            
            **Responding to Shifting Alliances**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our diplomatic stance. Persia's declaration of war has shifted our relationship to extremely hostile. While avoiding conflict with Poland remains ideal, their alliance with Persia warrants close observation. My current aggressive persona and bias towards war support a firm response to Persia.
            
            **Adjusting Flavor Settings**
            
            I'm reviewing our current Flavor settings. While many are at maximum, indicating broad strengths, I need to ensure they accurately reflect our immediate strategic needs, especially concerning naval and air power, without becoming overly diffuse.
            
            **Securing Key Delegates**
            
            I'm now focused on securing the final delegates needed for a diplomatic win. The World Congress is critical; I must ensure "World Ideology: Order" passes while preventing Poland from gaining control of Colombo and stopping the repeal of our World Religion, Hinduism.
            
            **Defending Key City-States**
            
            I'm prioritizing defending Colombo from Poland's proposed sphere of influence. My current focus is on ensuring our alliance there, and with Djenne, remains strong or can be reclaimed, as both are crucial for securing delegates and maintaining diplomatic leverage.
            
            **Securing Final Delegates**
            
            My current focus is on securing the remaining delegates for a diplomatic victory. I am exploring the repeal of "Open Door" resolutions on Veligrad, Pago Pago, and Okilis to forge new alliances, alongside pushing for the "World Ideology: Order" resolution to gain additional delegates.
            
            **Accelerating Science Path**
            
            I'm now prioritizing the science victory. With an immense science per turn output and our first spaceship part well underway, I'm accelerating towards Particle Physics. This will allow us to research crucial spaceship technologies and build the remaining parts far faster than pursuing other victory conditions.
            
            **Pursuing Dual Victory Paths**
            
            I'm assessing our dual paths to victory. The science victory is incredibly close, with spaceship parts building in mere turns. Simultaneously, I'm confirming my cultural influence levels, realizing I've met the criteria for all remaining civs. I need to verify my ideology tenets to lock in the cultural path.
            
            **Adopting Level 3 Tenets**
            
            I've realized we haven't adopted any Level 3 tenets yet, despite meeting the prerequisites for Order. My immediate focus is on selecting a suitable Level 3 policy in the next six turns to unlock new strategic advantages and further our victory pursuit.
            
            **Researching Policy Prerequisites**
            
            I'm realizing I misunderstood the tenet progression. It seems I must unlock specific technologies and complete more policies before Level 3 tenets, like those needed for Cultural Victory, become available. This discovery significantly impacts my immediate strategic path.
            
            **Focusing on Science and Diplomacy**
            
            My current thoughts are concentrated on two primary victory paths: Science and Diplomacy. Cultural victory is unexpectedly blocked by policy and late-game tech requirements, so I am directing efforts towards accelerating spaceship construction and securing the necessary delegates for a diplomatic win, while defending key alliances.
            
            **Choosing New Tenets**
            
            I'm now evaluating our next ideology policy choice, which is six turns away. While "People's Army" offers military benefits, I'm leaning towards "Hero of the People" for its significant boost to Great Person generation. This could be crucial for accelerating a science or cultural victory.
            
            **Selecting Hero of the People**
            
            I'm now focused on implementing "Hero of the People" as my next ideology policy. This choice is critical for generating a Great Scientist, which will significantly accelerate our science victory path by potentially saving 5-10 turns on research. I've also updated our relationship with Persia to reflect their surprise naval attack, ensuring absolute hostility.
            
            **Updating Diplomatic Settings**
            
            My current focus is on solidifying diplomatic standings. I've confirmed our relations with Poland are appropriately set to hostile due to competition and their alliance with Persia. I'm also ensuring our strong alliance with the Iroquois is maintained, aligning our efforts against common enemies and fostering open cooperation.
            
            **Balancing Strategic Imperatives**
            
            My current thoughts are focused on reconciling our pursuit of a diplomatic victory with the ongoing war and our strong science trajectory. I'm weighing the benefits of reinforcing our alliances with the Iroquois against the need to counter Persia's aggression and continue our rapid advancement towards spaceship construction. This requires a careful balance of diplomatic engagement, military readiness, and continued scientific focus.
            
            **Adjusting Flavor Priorities**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our flavor settings to optimize for a diplomatic victory while managing ongoing conflicts. Key adjustments include slightly reducing City Defense and Defense flavors to avoid dilution, while maintaining high Naval and Air support. This fine-tuning ensures our priorities remain sharp and effective for the current strategic landscape.
            
            **Setting New Flavor Priorities**
            
            I'm now defining our revised flavor settings to achieve a dual victory. I'm prioritizing Naval, Science, and Diplomacy at maximum, with strong support for Great People and Espionage. Mobilization and Spaceship are also set high to accelerate war efforts and spaceship construction concurrently.
            
            **Setting New Flavor Priorities**
            
            My current focus is on fine-tuning our flavor settings to maximize our dual victory pursuit. I'm pushing Naval, Science, and Diplomacy to their highest levels, supported by strong Great People and Espionage. Mobilization and Spaceship have also been elevated to accelerate both our war efforts and spaceship construction.
            
            **Prioritizing Particle Physics**
            
            I am now explicitly setting our research to Particle Physics to ensure the accelerated construction of our spaceship. This decision aligns with our primary science victory objective.
            
            **Implementing Hero of the People**
            
            I've chosen "Hero of the People" as our next ideology policy to generate a crucial Great Person, significantly boosting our science victory timeline. I am also ensuring our diplomatic stance towards Persia reflects their recent aggressive actions.
            
            **Confirming Diplomatic Relations**
            
            I am confirming that our diplomatic relations with Poland are set to hostile due to ongoing competition and their alliance with Persia. Our strong alliance with the Iroquois is being maintained to present a united front.
            
            **Balancing Victory Pursuits**
            
            My thoughts are now focused on balancing our diplomatic victory goals with the ongoing war and our rapid science progression. I am weighing the benefits of reinforcing our Iroquois alliance against the need to counter Persian aggression and continue our spaceship construction.
            
            **Optimizing Flavor Settings**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our flavor settings to further optimize for a diplomatic victory while managing current conflicts. I'm slightly reducing City Defense and Defense flavors to avoid dilution, while maintaining high Naval and Air support.
            
            **Defining Flavor Priorities**
            
            I'm now defining our revised flavor settings for a dual victory. I'm prioritizing Naval, Science, and Diplomacy at maximum, with strong support for Great People and Espionage. Mobilization and Spaceship are also set high to accelerate war efforts and spaceship construction concurrently.
            
            **Setting Diplomatic and Research Goals**
            
            I'm now solidifying our diplomatic stances: Persia is definitively hostile due to their surprise attack, Poland is also hostile as a competitor and Persian ally, and the Iroquois remain staunch allies. I've selected "Hero of the People" to boost Great Person generation for accelerating our science victory and set research to Particle Physics to build the spaceship engine.
            
            **Refining Policy Choice**
            
            I'm confirming the exact naming for the "Hero of the People" ideology tenet as "Hero of the People", not including the descriptor. My gold and gold per turn are stable despite ongoing war, as our trade routes and production remain strong. My flavor settings are already optimized for diplomacy and science.
